

##combat_modifiers## 


give_pursue_efficiency_true_magic_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_perk = slow_down_perk
		has_perk = speed_up_perk
		has_perk = war_magic_perk
	}

}

increase_pursue_efficiency_true_magic_trigger = {
	OR = {
		has_perk = speed_up_perk
		has_perk = war_magic_perk
	}
}

	OR = {
		has_perk = speed_up_perk
		has_perk = war_magic_perk
	}
}

		has_perk = slow_down_perk

	}
}

	}
}
